# v5 timeline: Screen_Recording_2026-10-31_at_16.20.37
60 fps analysis grid; 4609 frames; 1622x918 picture; classes: GRAPHICS 7, MIXED 19, LIVE 5.
Material: a screen recording of a web player (1622x936, variable frame rate; the top 18 rows, a black capture bar, are cropped away). The promo inside is 24 fps material on a 3:2 capture cadence (pictures 2-3 analysis frames apart, local_fps 23.6-24.6), so every duration carries +-1 frame of capture jitter plus the 2-3 frame quantisation of the pictures. The round speaker icon at the bottom right (x 1545-1613, y 837-905) is the player's overlay and is ignored; from f4531 the capture tool's dashed selection frame also sits on top (capture artifact, not design), and the capture has no pictures f4583-f4587. Footage is not described (rule 1); in S01-S07 the footage shots are only numbered: shot 1 f0-f269, shot 2 f270-f315, shot 3 f316-f354, shot 4 f355-f414, shot 5 f415-f649, shot 6 f650-f701.
Curve quality: shape rmse is fit_easing's RMSE on the normalised active window (it reads high on 24 fps material because the 2 % window ends fall between pictures); direct or raw-time rmse is the RMS of progress minus fit over the samples in the curve CSV. A few kept curves have both shape RMSEs above 0.05 (each named with its residuals in its block); moves that no fit could follow have values only and an UNCERTAIN easing.
Merged from six chunks: v5c1 (f0-f701), v5c2 (f702-f1599), v5c3 (f1600-f2414), v5c4 (f2415-f3041), v5c5 (f3042-f3886), v5c6 (f3887-f4608). Chunk edges checked at the merge on the overview sheets: f702 (c019), f2415 (c069) and f3042 (c086) are hard cuts that both neighbouring chunks found (sheets/overview_00672-00717.jpg, sheets/overview_02400-02445.jpg, sheets/overview_03024-03069.jpg); f1600 and f3887 fall inside held layouts (the same prompt box with its open mode menu on sheets/overview_01584-01629.jpg; the static two-card layout on sheets/overview_03840-03885.jpg and sheets/overview_03888-03933.jpg), so S11 (f1457-f1689) and S24 (f3710-f3921) are each stitched from two chunk blocks. Where chunks disagreed the frames decided: the S11 send button is a light rounded square with a dark up arrow (still type/v5c2-S4-ui_f01548.png), not round.

Boundaries: T01 f166-f200 star flash (soft; anchor c004) · T02 hard cut f270 (footage layer only, the title holds) · T03 f302-f323 radial zoom-through (soft; c008) · T04-T07 hard cuts f355, f415, f650, f702 · T08 hard cut f1120 on the brightest picture of a flare bloom · T09 hard cut f1362 · T10 f1442-f1470 whip-tilt with a hidden cut f1455|f1457 (soft; c048) · T11 hard cut f1690 at full speed of a push-in · T12 f1748-f1785 whip-pan between two footage shots (soft; c057) · T13 hard cut f1825 · T14 f1872-f1907 morph into a tree layout (soft; c061) · T15 f2052 and T16 f2415 hard cuts · T17 f2622-f2668 whip-tilt (soft; c074) · T18 f2740 and T19 f3042 hard cuts · T20 f3225-f3242 and T21 f3292-f3310 whip-tilts (soft; c093, c095) · T22 f3507 and T23 f3710 hard cuts · T24 f3905-f3937 melt-implode (soft; c106) · T25 f4000-f4025 white flash (soft; c113) · T26 f4035 and T27 f4057 hard cuts (the second cuts into motion) · T28 hard cut f4167 on matched upward motion · T29 f4355 and T30 f4588 hard cuts. 20 hard cuts and 10 soft transitions; 137 detector candidates decided (30 confirmed, 107 rejected, cuts.json). Segment edges follow the boundary rule: a soft transition [a, b] ends the previous segment at floor((a+b)/2). Not boundaries: the footage cuts inside a card window at f1987 and f2020 (S15) and the one-picture camera jumps f765|f767, f837|f840, f1000|f1002 (S08) and f1537|f1540 (S11).
Structure: footage with drifting kinetic captions and a chrome title (S01-S07); a product-UI sequence on near-black (S08, S10-S11); footage with captions (S09, S12-S13, S16, S19, S23); card, tree and canvas layouts that carry footage or photos (S14-S15, S17-S18, S20-S22, S24); a 3D space and a 3D card (S25-S28); a footage window (S29) and a hard-on end caption over dimmed footage (S30). LIVE: S04, S05, S07, S13, S31.
Patterns: elements arrive in mirrored pairs from opposite sides (headline lines in S08, S10, S20 and S22; streaks and titles in S24; cards in S15 and S24); two-phase entries drift in for 1-2 pictures and then snap with a hard ease-out (the S14 footage card, the S15 child cards); display type is set in per-line counter-tapers, one line growing and the other shrinking left to right (S10, S14-S15, S20, S22, S24); the caption layer is independent of the edit (it keeps drifting through the S12 whip); the 3D card sequence cuts two 22-frame close-ups (S26, S27) before a 110-frame continuous move (S28).
Rhythm: 109.96 BPM (32.7 frames per beat). Captions follow the voice-over, not the beat. Hard cuts are not beat-locked (offsets up to 16 frames). The camera settles of S17 land within 6 frames of a beat, and the main graphic starts of S25-S30 within 4 frames of a beat or half-beat.
Typefaces: UNCERTAIN: f0-f4608 because no original typeface could be identified from the grayscale pictures. Closest free: Inter (captions SemiBold/Bold, UI text Regular/Medium); Archivo wdth 125 at wght 800-900 or Unbounded 800-900 (the extended heavy caps headlines, silver vertical gradient fill, often counter-tapered in pairs); Inter Tight ExtraBold with DM Serif Display (the opening wordmark lockup, a brand mark); Archivo Narrow Bold, Montserrat Bold and Inter (text printed on the 3D card).
Captions: UNCERTAIN: every caption reveal and exit because blur and opacity change together and cannot be separated on the busy footage backgrounds; caption line first and last frames read off frame-numbered crops carry +-2 f.
Sources: numbers come from tracker CSVs in scratch/ (named by chunk: cap*, t1_*, t2_*, logo_*, v5c1fix_* and v5c1rev2_* for S01-S07, v5c2_* for S08-S11, v5c3_* for S11-S16, v5c4_* for S17-S19, v5c5_* for S20-S24, v5c6_* for S24-S31; the unprefixed rev*, rv4_* and p4_* files are review re-checks), from the fitted curves in curves/ (renamed to their final ids at the merge: 115 files, none dropped; the pre-merge set is kept in scratch/curves_premerge/ and the pre-merge cuts in scratch/cuts_premerge.json) and from frame-numbered stills in type/, which keep the chunks' provisional names: type/S01-* .. type/S06-* (= S01-S06), type/v5c2-S1-* .. type/v5c2-S4-* (= S08-S11), type/v5c3-P01-* .. type/v5c3-P06-* (= S11-S16), type/A-*, type/B-*, type/C-* (= S17, S18, S19), type/v5c5-S1-* .. type/v5c5-S5-* (= S20-S24), type/S-* (= S24-S30); type/chk-* are checks, not typography stills. Positions marked (still) or (crop) were read off those pictures.

**3D:** none.
**Graphic elements and UI:** text only until the star appears (f166).
**Speed changes:** none in the captions: the drift is constant from the first to the last visible picture; the reveal stagger follows the voice (about 1.6 f per letter, lines 11.7-20.6 f apart). The star decelerates into the centre (time constant about 4 f), grows fastest while it settles (f180-f185), and after it doubles the two stars' separation accelerates while their growth almost stops (see the transition).
**Transition out -> S02:** star flash (star split), 35 frames (f166-f200), scratch/v5c1fix_star_pair.csv: at f185 the star that dropped to the centre doubles into two identical, mirror-image 4-point stars (each spike has a bright and a dark bevel facet; the left star's dark facets point right, the right star's point left) that slide apart horizontally, symmetric about x 806-809 px, and leave past the left and right frame edges. Centre spacing (spike apexes at f185, spike-envelope centres on rows y20-y900 from f187): 0 (f180-f182, one star) -> 43 / 141 / 389 / 883 px = 2.7 / 8.7 / 24.0 / 54.4 %W at f185 / 187 / 190 / 192, x3.3 / x2.8 / x2.3 per picture (14 -> 49 -> 83 -> 247 px per frame: accelerating); at f195 the spike envelopes are cut by the frame edges, spacing >= 90 %W. The stars grow mainly before the split: per-star width on the centre row (union of both minus the spacing, >= 180 luma) 505 px (f182, one star) -> 680 (f185) -> 768 (f187) -> 788 px (f190) = 31.1 -> 41.9 -> 47.3 -> 48.6 %W; vertical tip-to-tip 625 px (f182) -> 848 px (f185, 92.4 %H), past the top and bottom edges from f187. The new composition shows from f185 in the notch between the doubled spike tips (gap between the top spikes on y60: 50 px at f187, 265 px at f190); on row y380 the gap between the stars' inner edges is 0 through f190, then 14.4 / 60.5 / 93.9 / 100 %W at f192 / 195 / 197 / 200 (x 709-942 / 315-1295 / 45-1567 px; row y540 within 2.5 %W). Frame mean luma (t1_luma_full.csv) 42.7 (f177) -> 58.6 (f182) -> 84.7 (f185) -> 104.0 (f187) -> 126.9 (f190) -> 137.8 (f192, peak) -> 93.6 (f195) -> 46.2 (f197) -> 41.5 (f200); the peak is the two large stars covering most of the frame, not a uniform flash: the dim footage stays visible between them (centre row dark between their inner tips at f192). Shares: the frame centre (symmetry axis of the split = later zoom origin) and the same static framing of footage shot 1, now dimmed; already present while the stars split: the serif line's chrome smear entering at the left edge (f187), the halftone orbit rings (first visible f190, drawn over the stars), a chrome shard rising at the lower right (f187-f190, see S02). Detail: detail/soft_166-200; crops/crop_560_0_500x160_00182-00192.jpg (spike doubling), crops/crop_0_360_1622x200_00190-00200.jpg (centre band). Curve: curves/v5.trans-star-flash.csv (opening on y380).
**Sound sync:** caption 1 first letter visible f27 (beat f23 +4, strong onset f21 +6); caption 1 line t50 f44 (beat f49 -5) and f56 (beat f49 +7); caption 2 first letter f108 (beat f112 -4), line t50 f121 (+9 vs f112) and f143 (+4 vs f139); star appears f168 (beat f171 -3); dim settled f177 (+6); star doubles f185 (beat f171 +14); luma peak f192 (beat f202 -10); stars gone f200 (beat f202 -2). The captions follow the voice-over, not the beat.
**Curves:** `curves/v5.text-caption-word-fade.i2.csv` spring {stiffness 53.0, damping 12.77, mass 1} rmse 0.032 (built 36 f); bezier(0.404, 0.438, 0.148, 0.582) D=33 rmse 0.060. `curves/v5.text-caption-line-fade-out.i2.csv` bezier(0.516, -0.392, 0.060, 0.618) D=31 rmse 0.033 (built 22 f); spring {192.5, 29.97, mass 1} rmse 0.036 (built 26 f). `curves/v5.fx-footage-dim.csv` bezier(0.170, -0.049, 0.412, 1.195) D=16 rmse 0.024; spring {342.4, 28.77, mass 1} rmse 0.066. `curves/v5.trans-star-flash.csv` (opening: gap between the two stars' inner edges on row y380, 0 -> 100 %W, rest at both ends f180-f190 / f200-f205) bezier(0.738, 0.734, 0.427, 1.000) D=9 rmse 0.047 (built 8 f, active f190-f200); spring {695.3, 40.26, mass 1} rmse 0.166. The separation itself (sep_pct in scratch/v5c1fix_star_pair.csv) is an accelerating ease-in that runs off the frame (no rest after), so it is given as values, not fitted.
**Uncertain:**
- UNCERTAIN: f160-f168 (start position and start frame of the star drop) because the star enters above the top edge; f166 shows only a glow at the edge, so the motion before f168 cannot be measured
- UNCERTAIN: f182-f197 (whether a jump cut hides under the flash) because the footage framing is identical before and after but the footage content differs after the white frames
- UNCERTAIN: f150 (caption 2 line 2 cap height) because line 2 has no capital; only the ascender height (40.0 px) was measured

| wordmark lockup (brand mark) appears | bold line: Inter Tight ExtraBold; serif line: DM Serif Display (closest free for both) / brand mark, original not identified | about 800 / 400 | bold line 14.4 (H 132.5 px); serif line about 13.1 (A 120 px incl. serifs) (f240) | normal | capitalised words | centred lockup; bold line x 265-1278 px (16.3-78.8 %W), top 310 px (33.8 %H); baseline pitch 157.5 px (17.2 %H) | 2 (icon + 1 word / 1 word) | line | the two lines enter from opposite edges as horizontal chrome smears (serif line from the left: head f187-f200, leading capital f197-f210; bold line from the right f202-f210) and resolve; then the whole lockup scales 0.88 -> 1.00 | serif head 13 f + capital 13 f, bold line 8 f + 2 f resolve, lockup settle 20 f (f215-f235); bold line starts 15 f after the serif head, 5 f after the serif capital appears | type/S02-logo_f00187.png, f00200, f00202, f00205, f00207, f00210, f00213, f00215, f00235, f00240 |

**3D:** the serif line shows an extruded chrome bevel for two pictures (f205-f206) before it goes flat (still); the split stars are bevelled (bright and dark facet on each spike, mirror images); the halftone rings are a pseudo-3D orbit rig: 3 circles (fitted radius 567 / 582 / 565 px = 35.0 / 35.9 / 34.8 %W, larger than half the frame height, so a ring whose major axis is near vertical, like ring S, runs past the top and bottom edges) whose planes are 50° / 68° / 90° apart (rings A-B / A-S / B-S), each plane's normal 54-59° from the spin axis; the whole rig spins about one fixed axis 51° from the view axis (screen projection: upper left to lower right, 28° from vertical) at a constant 1.5-1.7° per frame (about 100° measured f205-f268); the fit is orthographic (rms 14 px), so whether the top of the axis leans toward or away from the viewer is not decided by the ellipses (mirror solution, same rms); fitted ellipse centres differ between rings (edge-on pictures left out): A (756-809, 486-515), B (790-841, 424-451), S (840-893, 438-503) px, up to about 130 px apart, so there is either mild perspective or the rings are not exactly concentric; the rings cross in front of and behind the wordmark; no camera move on the graphics. Evidence: crops/v5c1rev2_rings_fit_00205-00268.jpg (each picture minus the median of f212-f268 with the three fitted ellipses), crops/crop_850_20_550x40_00212-00268.jpg (top band).
**Graphic elements and UI:** wordmark fill is a vertical luminance gradient (H stem luma 203 at the top -> 133 at the bottom, bright 1-2 px lower rim, f240) with a soft glow; one large 4-point sparkle with a long horizontal tail at the right end of the serif line (the line's head, in place from f200) and 3-4 small 4-point sparkles at its left (still f240); the small sparkles fly in from the frame edges as chrome lenses and turn into flat 4-point sparkles as they land (f197-f210), the large left one fades in at f212-f215 (see Movements); halftone-dot orbit rings of white dots, semi-transparent over the dimmed footage: each ring is a dotted band 18-27 px wide at half strength (1.1-1.7 %W), dot pitch 12-14 px (0.74-0.88 %W, autocorrelation), dots graded from 2-10 px isolated dots at the band edges to touching, merged dots in the core (scratch/v5c1rev2_rings_dots.csv).
**Speed changes:** the serif line's head decelerates into place first (128 -> 37 -> 7 px per frame, f187-f200) while its leading capital is still travelling (ease-out to f210), so the line is stretched along its travel and then contracts toward the head; the bold line's left end decelerates from 223 to 37 px per frame (f202-f210) and overshoots by 20-35 px (f212-f220); the serif line contracts 0.92 -> 0.88 (f207-f212) and holds (f212-f215) before the whole lockup grows (0.010 per frame f215-f217, fastest f217-f222 at 0.014-0.016 per frame, 0.004 per frame f222-f228, ease-out to f235); then a near-hold creep (0.055 % per frame from f237) that ramps into the accelerating exit in S03. The halftone ring rig spins at a constant rate (1.50 / 1.58° per frame in the windows f205-f240 / f240-f268: no speed change); the top crossing of ring S sweeps left at -5.33 px per frame (f222-f255), turns at f255-f257 and comes back at +2.59 px per frame (f257-f268): the turnaround comes from the projection of the steady spin (ring S keeps closing, b/a 0.26 -> 0.15, while its major axis keeps turning clockwise), not from a change of spin direction or speed.
**Transition out -> S03:** hard cut of the footage layer only at f270 (c005). The wordmark, sparkles and rings continue unchanged: template scale 1.015 at f268, f270, f272, f275 and centre (886, 388.5-389.5) px (`v5.layout-overlay-holds-across-cut`).
**Sound sync:** serif head enters f187 (beat f202 -15); serif capital first visible f197 (-5); bold line enters f202 (beat f202 0); serif line flat f207 (+5); lockup growth starts f215 (+13); wordmark settled f235 (beat f234 +1); footage cut f270 (beat f266 +4).
**Curves:** `curves/v5.logo-chrome-smear-in.i2.csv` (serif capital x, 8.0 -> 39.7 %W) spring {821.5, 55.18, mass 1} rmse 0.020 (built 12 f, active f197-f210); bezier(0.278, 0.669, 0.161, 0.642) D=9 rmse 0.068. `curves/v5.logo-chrome-smear-in.i3.csv` (bold line left end x, 88.5 -> 20.3 %W) spring {1907.6, 77.31, mass 1} rmse 0.012 (built 7 f, active f202-f210); bezier(0.456, 1.002, 0.376, 1.011) D=11 rmse 0.053. `curves/v5.logo-chrome-smear-in.i4.csv` (lockup scale 0.8775 -> 1.000, serif track) bezier(0.417, 0.627, 0.030, 0.918) D=21 rmse 0.037 (built 18 f, active f215-f235); spring {542.9, 46.89, mass 1} rmse 0.061. `curves/v5.logo-chrome-smear-in.csv` (bold line's own scale 0.86 -> 1.00, f210-f237) bezier(0.630, 0.441, 0.000, 0.948) D=26 rmse 0.0495 (built 21 f); spring {346.4, 34.69, mass 1} rmse 0.085 (built 17 f). Head of the serif line, sparkles and the separation of the split stars: values only (truncated or partly hidden paths). Halftone ring rig: no curve, it spins at a constant rate with no rest (linear, 1.5-1.7° per frame; samples in scratch/v5c1rev2_rings_rig_angle.csv and scratch/v5c1rev2_rings_ellipses.csv).
**Uncertain:**
- UNCERTAIN: f190-f204, f269-f316; ring A f190-f229 (halftone ring geometry outside f205-f268 and ring A before f230) because the ring measurement subtracts the median of the static shot-1 pictures f212-f268; before f205 the split stars and the entering wordmark cover the rings, after the footage cut at f270 the footage under them moves; ring A is edge-on or hidden behind the wordmark before f230, so its ellipse is not separable there (the spin is constant over f205-f268, but its continuation to f316 is seen on the sheets, not measured)
- UNCERTAIN: f205-f268 (lean of the ring rig's spin axis toward or away from the viewer) because the rigid fit uses only the projected ellipses (orthographic): a mirror solution with the axis top leaning the other way fits with the same rms (14 px)
- UNCERTAIN: f187-f226 (precision of the serif line's head and capital positions and of the sparkle paths) because smeared, stretched chrome shapes that template and threshold trackers do not hold; positions were read off 1x/2x ruler crops of single pictures (+-5-15 px, given per value); the serif head is hidden behind the left star f190-f195 and the capital before f197, so both paths start truncated
- UNCERTAIN: f195-f200 (star centres and size after f192) because the spike envelopes are cut by the frame edges (spacing only bounded, >= 90 %W at f195), and the halftone rings cross the spikes; the per-star width is measured only while both outer tips are in the frame (to f190)
- UNCERTAIN: f187-f197 (whether the slanted chrome shard at the lower right (1396, 745) f187 / (1472, 537) f190 is the right sparkle that appears at (1495, 438) at f197) because the element is hidden by the right star f192-f195 and changes shape
- UNCERTAIN: f200-f207 (whether the tiny bright marks at (230, 560) f200 and (400, 564) f202 are the lower-left sparkle arriving) because same row and direction as the sparkle that is at (490, 556) at f207, but only a few pixels large and not visible at f205

The fill fades fastest between f305 and f307 (stroke-centre luma -32 in one picture) while the scale is still slow; the streaks peak one picture after the last title picture (f316).
**Transition out -> S04:** zoom-through, 22 frames (f302-f323): the wordmark scales 1.035 -> 1.521 about (801, 455) px (f300-f312) while its fill fades to a glowing hollow outline (f300-f310) and radial streaks grow (streak length / radius 0.20 at f310, 0.23 at f312); its last picture f312 (middle letters still sharp outlines at x1.52) is held to f315; the footage is replaced between pictures f312 and f316 under the strongest streaks (0.47 of the radius at f316, longest 23.4 %W); the new shot then passes through one dark picture: frame mean luma 62.0 (f310) -> 59.0 (f312) -> 45.3 (f316) -> 20.8 (f318) -> 59.3 (f320) -> 73.9 (f323) -> 80.5 (f325) (t2_luma_full.csv). By f320 the vignette is gone (ratio 0.78) and a uniform residual dim lifts until the frame luma reaches its clear level at f325 (recovery curve; the radial blur is gone by f323). The top-left corner ROI (x0-300, y0-200) does not show the vignette because dark footage content sits there (10.0 at f297, 23.3 at f318, 10.4 at f323, t2_luma_tl.csv). Curves: curves/v5.trans-zoom-through-radial.csv (luma recovery), curves/v5.trans-zoom-through-radial.i2.csv (wordmark scale), curves/v5.trans-zoom-through-radial.i3.csv (fill fading to outline).

**Sound sync:** cut f415 (beat f422 -7); caption 3 first letter f420 (beat f422 -2); caption 3 line 2 t50 f446 (beat f452 -6); caption 4 first line f496 (beat f490 and strong onset f490, +6); later lines f508 (-15 vs f523), f520 (-3 vs f523), f536 (+13 vs f523), f548 (-8 vs f556); last line gone f596 (beat f588 +8); cut f650 (beat f660 -10).
**Curves:** same devices as S01; the fitted instances are caption 1's (`curves/v5.text-caption-word-fade.i2.csv`, `curves/v5.text-caption-line-fade-out.i2.csv`). Caption 3 and 4 contrast is disturbed by the push-in and pull-back, so no further fits.
**Uncertain:**
- UNCERTAIN: f415-f596 (caption 3 and 4 reveal/exit timing from contrast) because the background changes strongly (cut, push-in, pull-back), so the contrast measure is disturbed; timings were read off frame-numbered crops (caption 3 every frame, caption 4 every 4th frame, +-2 f)

- S07 · f650-f701 · LIVE · skipped (confirmed on sheets/overview_00624-00669.jpg, sheets/overview_00672-00717.jpg, detail/cut_c017_f00650/sheet.jpg, detail/cut_c018_f00683/sheet.jpg, detail/cut_c019_f00702/sheet.jpg); hard cut at f702 (c019) to the black GRAPHICS title card of S08 (beat f695 +7).

### S08 · f702-f1119 · GRAPHICS · 6.97 s
One UI layout (title card + prompt input + option chips + 3D action button) explored by a camera that snaps, whips and swings in 3D.
**Layers (front to back):** 3D extruded arrow cursor (f1031-f1117); exit bloom / horizontal anamorphic flare (f1107-f1117); 3D button (white extruded rounded bar, bold dark label + sparkle icon; bright fill x 38.3-62.2% W, y 76.9-84.9% H at f1007); dropdown menus (over the chips row and what lies below it); chips row (5 rounded chips, labels x 25.8-76.3% W, y 64.8-68.4% H at f1007; built one chip at a time: chip 1 pops in alone on f832 and the row to its right, x 576-1622 px, is still empty on f837; chip 2 slides in from the right edge between f862 and f867 while menu 1 opens (114 px of its own; the rest of its travel across the frame is the camera pull-back); each chip presses x0.81-0.83 and springs back when it is clicked open; chip 3 is first seen under the whip-pan blur on f922, moving with the row; chips 4-5 are first in frame at the snap-out f1002); input pill (x 16.5-83.9% W, y 50.7-61.4% H at f1007) with typed prompt; 2-line headline (ink x 19.7-80.1% W, y 24.3-45.3% H at f1007) with two sparkle ornaments (4-point star + horizontal streak tail) at the left of line 2 and the right end of line 2; click tile burst (glowing outlined and filled squares, behind the button and chips, f1085-f1100); background: near-black with a soft diagonal light falloff and faint thin curved strokes that move with the camera.

**3D:** the 3D button (extruded rounded bar, soft top light, bevel and drop shadow, matte-satin white) and the 3D cursor (chunky extruded arrow, glossy mid-gray with a dark rim/bevel) are true 3D objects; the cursor rotates freely (points left, up, right, down; one spin f1055-f1062 with motion blur). The camera swing f1022-f1067 tilts the whole UI plane into perspective (card top recedes, button foreshortened) and recoils after an overshoot. Depth of field: background strokes and far UI soft during the swing (f1027-f1045). Light: the button gains a white halo after the click (f1095-f1110) that grows into the horizontal bloom.
**Graphic elements and UI:** sparkle ornaments (4-point star + horizontal streak tail) parked at the line ends after their sweep; input pill (dark fill luma 14 on a 0-10 background, faint rim; 10.9% H tall at f1007) filled by luminous pixel squares; 5 chips (dark rounded pills, value label + chevron, the last one label + toggle switch; active chip = light outline and a brighter label; the row is built chip by chip: chip 1 pops, chip 2 slides in; a clicked chip presses to x0.81-0.83 about its centre, gets its light outline at the pressed size and springs back with a 1-4% overshoot before its menu is used); dropdown panels (rounded dark panel, rows with small outline icons + labels, selected row lighter with a check mark at the right; a newly chosen row presses to x0.79-0.81 and springs back, its highlight appearing at the pressed size while the old highlight lingers 1-3 pictures); click tile burst (outlined and filled squares of mixed size, ring expanding at constant speed, fading).
**Speed changes:** slide-ins fast (14 f) -> hold drift (+1.1 px/frame, f735-f747) -> camera accelerates (f747-f765) -> one-picture snap (f765|f767) -> 20 f expo settle; the same hold -> snap -> settle pattern repeats at f837|f840 (x2.9) and reversed at f1000|f1002 (x0.30); before the snap-out the content first stops at its lowest point (f980-f982), then accelerates upward for 18 f (about 1 px/frame at f982-f985 growing to 15 px/frame at f997-f1000, y only, scale 1.00) straight into the one-picture jump, the same ease-in-then-snap as snap 1. Chip 1 pops in 5 f (0.48 -> 1.00); chip 2 slides into place within one picture (89% of its 114 px between f862 and f865). Each chip activation is a dip and spring of the chip itself: press 5-8 f to x0.81-0.83, a 3-picture hold, release 5-7 f with a 1-4% overshoot (chip 1 f857-f887, chip 2 f925-f953). Each menu selection is a dip and spring of the chosen row: press 7-9 f to x0.79-0.81, release 9-10 f back to 1.00. The menu-1 opening is a camera pull-back x0.60 (f862-f880) with a rise (content up) that goes on slowly to f885 (chip 1 centre 50.0 -> 27.0 -> 18.4% H at f862 / f872 / f885); with no hold the camera then reverses (f885 -> f887) into a vertical whip-tilt (content down 32.1% H, f887-f913, peak 75-77 px per picture) that settles exactly on f913, the first picture of the horizontal whip pan: two whips chained, vertical then horizontal. Menu 2 opens and closes with pure tilts of 19% H each (content up f927-f951, down f957-f977; peaks 22.5 and 23.3 px/frame) separated by a 6 f hold at the top (f951-f957); the closing tilt ends at the lowest point (f980-f982) from which the snap-out approach starts. Whip pan: ease-in 9 f, blur peak 2-3 pictures, ease-out 10 f. 3D swing overshoots to 2.50 and recoils to 2.17 (speed reversal at f1055). The final push accelerates (chip scale steps +0.025, +0.025, +0.05, +0.075, +0.10 per picture) straight into the cut. The second dropdown is handled faster than the first (open -> select 12 f vs 24 f).
**Transition out -> S09:** hard cut, 0 frames (f1119|f1120), preceded by a 10-frame flare bloom (`v5.fx-flare-bloom-out`) over an accelerating push-in through the clicked button (`v5.cam-push-in-accelerate`); last picture f1117: frame washed bright (mean 128.8) with a horizontal light band through the centre and the cursor/button blown up; first picture f1120: footage at normal exposure (57.7) with the first caption already fading in at the left. Shares only luminance energy, no shape.
**Sound sync:** cut f702 = beat f695 +7 (no onset detected between f645 and f828); line 2 f716 = beat f727 -11; snap 1 f766 = beat f761 +5; typing start f786 = beat f796 -10; chip 1 pop f832 = beat f829 +3; snap 2 f838 = weak onset f837 +1 (beat f829 +9); chip 1 press f857 = beat f861 -4; chip 2 slide-in f862 = beat f861 +1; selection 1 press start f880 = beat f893 -13, its deepest picture + label swap f887 = strong onset f892 -5 (beat f893 -6), which is also the start of the vertical whip-tilt; whip peak f921 = beat f926 -5; chip 2 press f925-f927 = beat f926 -1 / +1; selection 2 deepest f951 = beat f954 -3, label swap f953 = weak onset f953 0 (beat f954 -1); whip-tilt back start f957 = beat f954 +3; snap-out approach start f982 = beat f982 0; snap-out jump f1001 = weak onset f1003 -2; 3D swing start f1022 = strong onset f1023 -1; click f1081 = beat + strong onset f1079 +2; burst f1085 +6; cut f1120 = beat f1117 +3.

**3D:** the logo spheres are rendered glossy 3D balls (dark glass, white logo glyph inside, specular top-left highlight); they keep their size while flying (no depth travel measured); no camera 3D.
**Graphic elements and UI:** flare streak, 4-point sparkle stars (large; they ride the streak to the line ends, fly to opposite corners in step and park there), a faint wireframe of orbit ellipses tumbling in the background.
**Speed changes:** fast entries (13 f slides, 25 f sphere fly-in, 20 f ease-in-out star flight f1382-f1402 synchronous with the headline lift) -> 20 f hover (f1412-f1431) with a slow inward drift of the foreground (about 0.7 px/frame at the corners) -> accelerating exit (fall + tilt) into the whip. The background wireframe never stops: it tumbles at 2-9 px/frame through the entries, the hover and the exit.
**Transition out -> S11:** whip (vertical whip-tilt), 29 frames (f1442-f1470), hidden cut f1455 -> f1457; shares: the three logo spheres keep falling across the cut (same objects, same downward direction, vertical blur) and the camera keeps tilting down (content up) on both sides: the headline leaves through the top while the prompt card arrives from below; the content speed rises to 15.3 px/frame on the last outgoing picture and is 29 px/frame on the first incoming one, so the speed peak sits on the hidden cut; curves: curves/v5.trans-whip-tilt.i2.csv (outgoing ease-in, f1433-f1455) + curves/v5.trans-whip-tilt.i3.csv (incoming deceleration, f1457-f1472); detail: detail/soft_1442-1470.
**Sound sync:** cut f1362 = weak onset f1357 +5; line 1 slide f1367 = beat f1373 -6; line 2 f1375 = beat f1373 +2; spheres and star flight f1382 = beat f1373 +9; stars settled f1407 = beat f1407 0; fall start f1433 = weak onset f1439 -6; whip fast phase f1442 = beat (and weak onset) f1439 +3; hidden cut f1456 = beat f1472 -16.
**Curves:** `curves/v5.logo-sphere-fly-in.csv` bezier(0.179, 0.348, 0.110, 0.995) D=31, shape 0.044; spring {211.7, 30.59} shape 0.091. `curves/v5.text-lift-to-header.i2.csv` bezier(0.652, -0.127, 0.153, 1.236) D=38, shape 0.018; spring {191.1, 20.38} shape 0.109. `curves/v5.shape-flare-streak-sweep.i3.csv` bezier(0.685, 0.282, 0.217, 0.639) D=18, shape 0.029, direct 0.011 (bottom-left star y). `curves/v5.trans-whip-tilt.i2.csv` bezier(0.375, 0.003, 1.000, 0.357) D=23, shape 0.060, direct 0.008 (ends in the hidden cut).
**Uncertain:**
- UNCERTAIN: f1375-f1383 (S10 line-2 slide easing) because only 3 blurred pictures
- UNCERTAIN: f1362-f1456 (rotation axis and angular rate of the background wireframe as a 3D object) because no single in-plane rotation fits its strokes (rigid-rotation R2 0.00-0.22); stroke speeds and axis turn rates are given instead

### S11 · f1457-f1689 · GRAPHICS · 3.88 s
Prompt-box UI: the logo spheres drop onto the chip row and collapse into a glow that vanishes while the camera pushes in (x1.15), a prompt types, the camera pulls back to the landing framing, whips to the right end of the box (its jump picture also pushes in x1.25 and lifts the card 13.2% H) and opens the mode dropdown, where the chosen option presses and springs back. Then the dropdown collapses into its chip while the box group creeps and snaps down-left, the box slides left to centre the send button, the pressed button throws a concentric ring burst and a square-tile sweep, and an accelerating push-in on the button runs into the hard cut T11.
One segment across the v5c2/v5c3 chunk edge: the same prompt-box view with the open mode menu continues over f1584-f1629 (sheets/overview_01584-01629.jpg), so the two chunk blocks are merged here (f1457-f1599 measured in v5c2, f1600-f1689 in v5c3).
**Layers (front to back):** f1457-f1599: falling logo spheres -> one glowing blob -> point (f1457-f1482, gone f1485); dropdown menu (2 options, f1560 on); prompt card: rounded dark panel (fill about luma 25-30, lighter 1 px rim) with typed prompt (top left), bottom row (+ button, icon chip with icon, text chip + chevron), right end (mode label with icon, light rounded-square send button with a dark up arrow); background near-black with soft diagonal light. f1600-f1689: fx (ring outlines, glowing square tiles, button glow) > dropdown panel (2 option rows, each a rounded icon tile + 3-word title + 5/6-word subtitle; second row highlighted with a check mark) > prompt-box contents (label chip with a small glyph icon + 3-word label; send button = light rounded square with a dark up arrow) > prompt-box panel (dark rounded rect, luma ~24) > background (near-black, faint diagonal light streak; static: bottom-band correlation 0.999 at zero shift while the box moves).

**3D:** none (flat UI; the ring rotation is 2D; the last UI picture f1687 shows a bulge warp of the box outline under the push-in).
**Graphic elements and UI:** prompt card, + button, icon chip (icon), text chip with chevron, mode label with icon, light rounded-square send button with a dark up arrow, 2-option dropdown with check mark and highlighted row. From f1600: prompt box with label chip and send button; 2-option dropdown with icon tiles and a check mark on the selected row; ring outlines with a soft glow; square tiles with a glow; the selected option is the one chosen at f1572-f1587.
**Speed changes:** the whip lands at full speed on its first picture (29 px/frame, f1457) and decelerates to 4-6 px/frame by f1470-f1472; without a hold a separate push-in re-frame takes over (x1.15 and up/right, 79% of the scale in 5 f, then a 10 f creep, f1472-f1487) while the merged glow collapses with an accelerating shrink (f1470-f1485); typing at a constant rate (faster than in S08); the pull-back re-frame (f1493-f1517) overlaps the typing and undoes the push-in; the second whip has a longer settle than the S08 whip, and its jump picture (f1537 -> f1540) also carries a one-picture x1.25 push-in with a 13.2% H rise of the card, which the S08 whip does not have (its chip-label template keeps y 466 px and scale 0.99-1.03 through the blur f913-f925, scratch/v5c2_A_whip1_scale.csv); the option press dips 8 f and springs back 7 f. From f1597: group move = slow creep (f1597-1615) then a 13-frame snap (speed x5); the dropdown collapse accelerates under the snap (group-relative width 0.99 -> 0.90 -> 0.73 -> <= 0.29 -> 0.03 at f1615/1617/1620/1622/1626) and is gone when the snap lands (f1628); box slide eases in and out (25 f); ring burst eases in over 2 pictures, then grows at a near-constant logarithmic rate (x1.64-1.69 per picture); push-in accelerates to the cut (scale step per picture 1.20 -> 1.31 -> 1.60).
**Transition out -> S12:** hard cut at f1690 (0 frames). Last UI picture f1687: light send button 18.9 %W wide (124 px at rest), centred 50.0 %W, 47.5 %H, zooming in at full speed; first footage picture f1690: full-frame footage that itself keeps zooming in (inside the footage, x0.86-0.92 per picture until ~f1712); shared: zoom-in direction only; mean luma 35 -> 99.
**Sound sync:** card arrival f1457 = beat f1472 -15; glow collapse start f1470 = beat f1472 -2; push-in re-frame start f1472 = beat f1472 0; glow gone f1485 = beat f1472 +13; typing f1489 = beat f1504 -15; whip-pan peak f1538 = beat + strong onset f1537 +1 (the re-frame step f1537|f1540 sits on it: 0 / +3); dropdown f1557 = weak onset f1548 +9 (beat f1570 -13); option press start f1572 = beat f1570 +2; its deepest picture + label swap f1580 = weak onset f1586 -6 (beat f1570 +10); snap/collapse f1615 +12 to beat f1603; dropdown gone f1628 -7 to beat f1635; slide start f1630 -5 to beat f1635; press f1655 -8 to beat f1663; ring growth peak ~f1662 -1 to beat f1663; push-in start f1667 +4; cut f1690 -5 to beat f1695 (beat period 32.7 f).

Fill: vertical metallic gradient (luma ~235 at the top of a stem -> ~165 at the bottom) with a bright rim along the base; no glow halo.
**3D:** headline drawn in perspective (static size taper); cursor is a beveled 3D arrow with a dark outline and drop shadow, tilting in flight; during the morph the headline skews/tilts back while shrinking.
**Graphic elements and UI:** footage card (masked window with live footage, rim + glow), glints (4-point star + hairline streak), cursor, halftone-dot corner patches in the background.
**Speed changes:** words 2-3 decelerate from >=141 px/f to 7.7 px/f within one picture; card fly-in changes pace mid-way: it drifts in at ~33 px/f in x (~23-31 px/f in y) for 2 pictures (f1825-1830), snaps at ~176 px/f in x between f1830 and f1832 (5x faster) and then eases out to rest by f1853; the cursor flies in decelerating all the way (~104 px/f between f1835 and f1837, 2 px/f by f1850-1853), rests f1855-1862 while its tilt runs out (0 deg at f1860), then winds up (-21 px) before a fast swoop; once landed the whole group recedes at a constant -0.032 %/f (f1837-1870).
**Transition out -> S15:** morph, 36 frames (f1872-f1907), midpoint f1889. Shared element: the footage card shrinks 1.0 -> 0.34 (undershoot 0.31 at f1885) and rises from 59.3 to 20.1 %H at x 49.9 %W to become the parent node; most of it happens in 2 frames (0.75 -> 0.34, f1880-1882). Headline 1 shrinks with it (65.0 -> 45.7 %W by f1880), tilts and blurs out by f1885. A thin light line first stretches from both card sides (right end following the cursor, f1875-1882), then the tree bracket draws out from the parent node (runs 0 -> ~130-145 px = 8-9 %W each side, f1882-1892). A field of glowing semi-transparent squares flashes in a ring around the parent and spreads over the background (region luma 13.4 -> 78.5 f1887-1892, held to f1895, back to 15.6 by f1905; the right side peaks later, 59.5 at f1895). Child cards: the middle card's top rim comes up over the bottom edge first (f1885), the right card's rim follows at f1890, and the left card appears high in frame at f1890 and drops into place; the middle and right cards drift in, snap up and settle by f1905 / f1907 (the right one picture behind the middle), the left settles by f1902 (see S15). Cursor lands at (88.0, 42.8) %. Detail: `detail/soft_1872-1907`. Curve: `curves/v5.trans-morph-tree.csv`.
**Sound sync:** cut f1825 +15 to beat f1810 (+16 to strong onset f1809); words landed f1830 -12 to beat f1842; card readable f1832 -10; cursor enters f1835 -7 to beat f1842; cursor lands f1855 +13 to beat f1842 (-9 to strong onset f1864); cursor departure f1862 -2 to strong onset f1864; morph start f1872 -2 to beat f1874; morph snap f1882 +8.

From ~f1917 (headline settled) the layout's only motion is the slow recede (scale 1.000 -> 0.957 by f2050 about the frame centre) while footage plays inside the four windows; the cursor and the background do not scale. The halftone-dot grid of the background stays put, but the brightness of its lit patches drifts slowly on its own (dot-energy centroid of the top-left patch x 107 -> 201 -> 95 px over f1905-1987-2035; see Uncertain below).
**Speed changes:** child cards change pace mid-move: the middle card drifts in at 10-30 px/f for 2 pictures (f1885-1890), snaps at ~187 px/f (f1890-1892) and eases out hard to rest by f1905; the right card repeats it one picture later (1 entry picture, snap f1892-1895, rest f1907); the left card shows only an ease-out (37 px/f at f1890-1892 down to 1 px/f, rest f1902); constant type-on rate; the tracking settle of headline 2 eases out over 15 f (f1902-1917); no static hold: the whole layout recedes at a constant -0.033 %/f from f1892 at the latest to the hard cut, which lands at that constant speed.

### S17 · f2415-f2645 · MIXED · 3.85 s
Graphic canvas (black, halftone-dot background) with a typed headline and three rounded cards that carry photographic stills (no motion inside the stills, hence MIXED). A 3D camera explores one curved layout: headline types on, lifts to a header, card 1 rises in, three whip pans and a pull-back reveal the full row; the segment ends inside the vertical whip.

**Layers (front to back):** headline (white, luma 249) · cards with gray UI labels under them (label luma max 143-156) · halftone-dot beams in the gaps between cards (part of the card canvas) · wide card on the same canvas below the row (its top edge enters the frame bottom at f2630-2640) · screen-locked background: black (luma 0-2) with halftone-dot vignettes in the top-right and bottom-left corners (luma <= 25; region mean 3.7-5.3 before and after every whip, so it does not move with the camera). No halftone ribbons in S17 until the tilt's peak: the right margin (x 1400-1540 px, y 300-600 px) reads max luma <= 4 on every picture f2415-f2645 and the left margin (x 0-200 px, y 250-650 px) 0 whenever no card covers it, e.g. f2617-f2645 (`scratch/v5c4_bg_mr.csv`, `v5c4_bg_ml.csv`); the first faint ribbon dots show on f2645, the last picture of S17 (see the transition).

**3D:** one curved layout seen by a 3D camera: the cards sit on the inside of a cylinder around the camera, so side cards turn toward the centre (card 1 at f2630: outer edge 389-400 px tall vs inner edge 359-372 px, ~7% taller; card 2 at f2575 seen from the side with its outer edge taller). The headline shares the curve (f2470: letters 79 -> 87 px tall left to right, baseline -5.5 deg). Camera moves: yaw + push-in x1.26 (whip 1), yaw (whip 2), yaw + push-in x1.22 (whip 3), dolly out x0.58 + yaw back (pull-back), tilt down (transition). Moderate perspective; no depth of field (all cards sharp at rest), only motion blur on the 2 fastest pictures of each move; flat matte cards, no lighting change.
**Graphic elements and UI:** portrait cards, aspect 0.80-0.83 (w/h) when frontal (card 2: 452 x 543 px at f2512; 308 x 385 px at f2630); corner radius 7-7.5% of card width (34 px at 452 px wide, 22 px at 308 px; corner inset profile on type/A-label_f02512.png); no border, no shadow. Halftone-dot beams: horn/bow-tie cones of dots bridging the card gaps, brightest at the receiving card, an internal bright cluster oscillates (beam 2: 4.1% W swing over ~27 f); each beam enters as a small dotted glint 3 f before the seam of the card it leads to (beam 1 f2482 -> card 2 f2485; beam 2 f2537 -> card 3 f2540), then grows into a horn whose wide end sits on that seam. Background halftone vignette fixed to the screen.
**Speed changes:** type-on at a constant 2 letters per picture flows straight into the lift (no hold at f2425). Camera rhythm: whip 1 (25 f) - hold 13 f - whip 2 (30 f) - hold 22 f - whip 3 (~33 f) - hold ~17 f - pull-back (35 f) - 5 f - tilt (46 f). Durations grow with travel (25 -> 34 -> ~38% W, the last UNCERTAIN because card 2 leaves the frame); every move is a hard ease-in-out (bezier x1 0.85-0.93, x2 0.00-0.11): slow creep, a 2-picture blur burst, long settle. Holds keep a residual drift (0.5-0.9 px/f). The last two moves chain almost without a hold, so the pace accelerates into the transition.
**Transition out -> S18:** vertical whip (canvas tilt), 47 frames (f2622-f2668); one rigid canvas: the 3-card row and headline slide up out of frame while the wide card and its label slide up from below (row labels still visible at the top edge on f2650-f2652); background not the same: the black ground and the screen-locked corner vignettes carry on unchanged, but a new layer of halftone-dot ribbons that belongs to the next layout appears inside the move (first faint dots f2645 where card 1 has just left, clear dots f2647 in margins that were pure black through S17, filled by ~f2668) and rises with the canvas while it brightens (see S18, `v5.bg-ribbon-rise-in`); motion direction up, peak speed ~131 px/f (14% H/f) at f2645-2647 with heavy vertical blur f2642-2650; curve: curves/v5.trans-whip-tilt.csv
**Sound sync:** tempo 110 BPM (32.7 f/beat). Cut f2415 +14 (half-beat, between beats). Type-on end f2425 -13. Card 1 enters f2437 -1 and lands f2442 +4 (on beat 2438). Whip settles land near beats: whip 1 f2467 -3, whip 2 f2510 +6, whip 3 f2565 -4; blur peaks fall between beats (f2453 +15, f2496 -8, f2547 +11). Beam glints: beam 1 f2482 +12, beam 2 f2537 +1 (not consistently beat-locked). Pull-back blur peak f2602 +1 (on beat), its settle f2617 +16 (half-beat). Tilt start f2622 -12. No strong onset within 150 f.
**Curves:** `curves/v5.text-type-swing-in.csv` bezier(0.000, 1.072, 0.000, 0.638) D=7; spring {15081.9, 39.77, mass 1} rmse 0.133; bezier rmse 0.034 · `curves/v5.text-lift-to-header.csv` bezier(0.720, 0.290, 0.187, 1.034) D=21; spring {290.6, 26.57} rmse 0.136; bezier rmse 0.016 · `curves/v5.cam-whip-pan.csv` (whip 2, card 1 label track) bezier(0.919, 0.197, 0.049, 0.893) D=35; spring {198.2, 23.72} rmse 0.173; bezier rmse 0.015 · `curves/v5.cam-whip-pan.i3.csv` (whip 1, card 1 label track) bezier(0.854, 0.004, 0.000, 0.792) D=27; spring {634.4, 50.91} rmse 0.128; bezier rmse 0.017 · `curves/v5.card-seam-reveal.csv` bezier(0.737, 0.008, 0.304, 0.894) D=26; spring {194.0, 22.79} rmse 0.104; bezier rmse 0.018 · `curves/v5.cam-whip-pullback.csv` bezier(0.887, 0.276, 0.110, 0.996) D=42; spring {104.8, 15.92} rmse 0.310; bezier rmse 0.019 · `curves/v5.trans-whip-tilt.csv` bezier(0.928, 0.138, 0.000, 0.974) D=60; spring {196.6, 26.50} rmse 0.226; bezier rmse 0.044

**3D:** cards are flat rounded panels placed in 3D with individual tilts (card 1 left edge leans 9.1 deg at f3112: x 173 at row 250, x 125 at row 550; its top edge is still almost level there, 0.9 deg, as the turn has just begun). From f3107 the two outer cards turn as a mirrored pair (top edges +-9.8 deg, card 1's bottom edge 6.2 deg in the hold, `v5.card-mirror-tilt`) while their side edges do not follow (card 1's left-edge lean 9.1 deg at f3112 -> 4.6 deg at f3150, rows 250-400), so it reads as a 3D turn rather than a flat rotation; rotation axis UNCERTAIN. They turn back to +-1.3 deg during the pull-back (f3177-3202); card 2 stays level (0.0 deg) throughout. The send button keeps a 7 deg tilt from its pop-in to the whip. The headline plane foreshortens during the tilt (letter height x0.74). No 3D objects.
**Graphic elements and UI:** 3 rounded picture cards (card 2: 23.5 % W x 43.7 % H at f3112, corner radius about 35 px = 2.2 % W measured from the edge inset), each with a UI label below; dark prompt pill (luma about 25 on black) with typed text; send button (rounded square with an arrow icon, tilted 7 deg; scales in from a speck and squashes to 0.77 on the click); arrow cursor with dark outline; ring of glowing outlined and filled square tiles on click; sparkle (4-point star) with a light streak at the headline's end; halftone dot gradient in the corners.
**Speed changes:** fast-in liquid fly-in (10 f) -> slow drift/shrink (20 f) -> two-phase tilt (card 1 top: 26 % H in 8 f, then 42 % H in 7 f, then 5.7 % H over 13 f) -> slow reframe (38 f) with the outer cards turning +-9.8 deg inside it (f3107-3130, accelerating to an abrupt stop); the headline's line 2 holds cut off at the top edge through the first 23 f of the reframe (f3107-3129) and then leaves in 2 pictures (3.5 % H in one step, f3130-3132; gone f3135) -> word cadence about 8 f -> in-out pull-back (32 f, fast middle f3185-3195) that also turns the outer cards back (f3177-3202) while the send button scales in over 2 pictures -> 15-frame hold with the cursor -> press (5 f; cursor and button squash together) -> whip (17 f).
**Transition out -> S21:** whip-tilt, 18 frames (f3225-f3242); the board and the next layout sit on one rigid canvas 880 px (95.9 % H) apart: the board leaves upward while the curved footage panel arrives from below; shares: the UI cursor stays fixed on screen at (80.9 % W, 92.8 % H) through the whole move, same upward motion, same dark halftone background; curve: `curves/v5.trans-whip-tilt.i4.csv`; detail `detail/soft_3225-3242`.

**Speed changes:** lands at the whip's speed, 45-frame hold with a slow push-out, the cursor accelerates out, the exit accelerates hard (bezier x1 0.94); at the exit's fastest pictures the outline goes soft and wobbles (f3297-3302).
**Transition out -> S22:** whip-tilt exit, 19 frames (f3292-f3310): the panel rises out of the top edge with vertical motion blur and the camera lands on an empty dark canvas where the S22 labels then form in place (first blob f3305, while the panel's last sliver still occupies y 0-6 % H). Unlike the arrival (f3225-3242, outline straight and sharp at the same speed) the panel is not rigid on the way out: from f3297 its edges go soft (ramps 2 -> 4-7 px) and bulge out where the footage is bright, so the sides wave and lean by up to 14.5 px and the lower edge turns lumpy and sloped (f3297-3307, `v5.fx-liquid-edge-wobble`; crops `crops/crop_210_0_100x918_03290-03307.jpg`, `crops/crop_1320_0_100x918_03290-03307.jpg`, dense sheet `detail/dense_03290-03310_00.jpg`, stills `type/v5c5-S2S3-exit_f03295.png`, `_f03297`, `_f03300`, `_f03302`), so the exit already looks liquid before the S22 labels form from liquid blobs. Shares: upward motion, dark halftone background; nothing graphic carries over; curve: `curves/v5.trans-whip-tilt.i5.csv`; detail `detail/soft_3292-3310`.
**Sound sync:** panel landed f3242 -5 (beat f3247, weak onset f3247); cursor exit f3263 -13 (beat f3276); exit start f3292 -12 (beat f3304); outline wobble from f3297 -7 (beat f3304); transition midpoint f3301 -3.
**Curves:** `curves/v5.trans-whip-tilt.i5.csv` bezier(0.942, 0.117, 0.097, 0.877) D=19; spring {2858.2, 107.20}; rmse 0.050 (direct residual <= 0.008). The outline wobble has no curve (a shape change over 3-5 pictures while the panel leaves; readings in `scratch/v5c5_BC_panel_outline.csv`).
**Uncertain:**
- UNCERTAIN: f3297-f3307 (mechanism of the exit outline wobble) because the edge offset follows the footage brightness (r up to +0.96), which a luminance-driven displacement and a glow bleeding from bright footage would both give; only the outline is measurable
- UNCERTAIN: f3297-f3302 (corner shape of the footage panel during the exit) because vertical motion blur and the soft, brightness-dependent edges hide the corner radius
- UNCERTAIN: f3297-f3302 (panel width during the exit) because the side edges sit up to 14.5 px (left) and 6.4 px (right) further out in rows where the footage is bright, so a width read at f3300 depends on the rows used (up to 21 px = 1.9 % of the panel width)

**3D:** each card and title swings from flat into a fixed perspective taper as it lands (`v5.3d-perspective-swing`; starts f3730, 3732, 3740, 3745; 12-18 frames each; crops `crops/crop_0_280_1622x638_03727-03765.jpg`, `crops/crop_40_180_760x150_03740-03760.jpg`, `crops/crop_780_590_760x140_03740-03765.jpg`). The cards' inner sides shorten (card 1's right side 427 -> 400 px while its left edge reaches about 482 px at f3760; card 2's left side 425 px against 445 px at its right) and the titles' inner ends grow (title 1 73 -> 97 px left to right, title 2 60 -> 38 px), so the titles taper against their cards. The x extents stay put (cards within 1-2 px, title 1 within 2 px, title 2 -13 px), so it reads as a keystone taper without visible horizontal foreshortening; rotation axis and perspective distance UNCERTAIN. In the hold and the exit the layout is static in that fixed perspective (no further 3D motion).
**Graphic elements and UI:** 2 rounded footage cards, 2 thin light streaks with 4-point sparkle stars (the stars stay at the titles' outer ends), dark background with faint curved light ribbons that drift continuously (2-7 px per frame at the measured columns; the median Farneback flow of this sparse, low-contrast layer reads 0, so it was tracked by column-profile peaks, `scratch/v5c5_E_bg_ribbons.csv`; crop `crops/crop_700_700_845x218_03752-03884.jpg`). At rest and in the exit: two footage cards (rounded corners, no stroke), two headlines, two sparkle marks; during the exit they behave as one flattened layer.
**Speed changes:** entrances staggered 5 / 10 / 5 frames; card 1 (1.9 % H) and both titles (0.9 and 1.3 % W) land with an overshoot, card 2 without; each element starts its 12-18 f perspective swing as it lands (f3730, 3732, 3740, 3745: stagger 2 / 8 / 5), so the swings ripple through the layout right behind the entrances; then cards and titles hold for 143 frames (f3762-f3904, to the melt) while the background ribbons keep drifting at a slow, even pace. Exit: static hold -> ease-in warp/bloom (10 f) -> stepped collapse (shrink f3917-f3922, flare pulse f3922-f3925, shrink to a point f3925-f3930) -> flare fade to f3937.
**Transition out -> S25:** melt-implode, 33 frames (f3905-f3937); shares: the frame centre (the blob collapses onto x 50%, y 50%, where S25's cranes later converge and its rings are born); S25's first element (a small crane at x 47%, y 71-76%) is already visible at f3932 while the flare dies; curve: curves/v5.trans-melt-implode.csv; detail: detail/soft_3905-3937.
**Sound sync:** cut in f3710 -16 (beat f3726); card 1 f3717 -9; card 2 f3722 -4; lower streak f3725 -1; title 1 f3732 +6; title 2 f3737 +11; swings start card 1 f3730 +4, card 2 f3732 +6 (beat f3726), title 1 f3740 +14, title 2 f3745 -15 (beat f3760). Strong onsets f3772, f3823, f3829, f3884 fall in the hold: no designed element moves on them, and the background ribbons drift through them without a jump; melt start f3905 = beat f3912 -7; bloom peak f3915 = beat f3912 +3; flare pulse f3925 = +13; flare gone / first trail f3940 = beat and strong onset f3940 (0).
**Curves:** `curves/v5.card-rise-drop-in.i3.csv` bezier(0.006, 1.057, 0.478, 1.176) D=17; rmse 0.012 · `curves/v5.card-rise-drop-in.i4.csv` spring {1906.6, 88.82}; rmse 0.068 (bezier(0.094, 0.807, 0.000, 0.948) D=12, shape rmse 0.111, direct residual <= 0.001) · `curves/v5.shape-flare-streak-sweep.csv` bezier(0.233, 1.211, 0.388, 1.050) D=19; rmse 0.017 · `curves/v5.text-line-slide-overshoot.csv` bezier(0.254, 1.023, 0.272, 1.101) D=20; spring {828.2, 46.89}; rmse 0.009 · `curves/v5.text-line-slide-overshoot.i2.csv` bezier(0.000, 0.710, 0.000, 1.140) D=18; rmse 0.111 (direct residual <= 0.003) · `curves/v5.3d-perspective-swing.csv` (title 2) bezier(0.652, 0.292, 0.050, 0.707) D=16; spring {780.1, 62.29}; rmse 0.021 · `curves/v5.3d-perspective-swing.i5.csv` bezier(0.798, 0.351, 0.123, 0.408) D=12; rmse 0.000 (5 pictures) · `curves/v5.3d-perspective-swing.i3.csv` bezier(0.706, 0.795, 0.180, 0.737) D=13; spring {444.1, 36.60}; rmse 0.054 · `curves/v5.3d-perspective-swing.i4.csv` bezier(0.844, 0.835, 0.000, 0.548) D=14; spring {326.0, 30.80}; rmse 0.068. The four swing curves are one device (12-18 f in-out eases on different elements). The four entrance curves were re-measured on swing-free readings (card 1's centre, card 2's right-end top edge, title 1's constant-width band edge, title 2's ink centre): the earlier edge readings counted part of the swing as overshoot (card 1 2.4 -> 1.9 % H, card 2 1.6 % H -> none, title 1 1.3 -> 0.9 % W, title 2 1.9 -> 1.3 % W). · `curves/v5.trans-melt-implode.csv` spring {stiffness 259.6, damping 26.47, mass 1} built 15 f (shape rmse 0.045, raw-time 0.041); bezier(1.000, 1.385, 0.409, 0.249) D=11 (shape 0.084, raw-time 0.021).

- UNCERTAIN: f3747-f3760 (title 2 slide overshoot magnitude) because the swing narrows the title by 13 px while it settles; the pivot of that narrowing is unknown, so the ink-centre overshoot (1.3 % W) carries +-0.4 % W

- UNCERTAIN: f3915-f3920 (blob rotation during the collapse) because 3 pictures; image-moment angle only

### S25 · f3922-f4012 · GRAPHICS · 1.52 s
**Layers (front to back):** a crane passing very close to the camera (enters at the upper left at f3957, x 13-44%, y 0-35%; its trail becomes a chain of large soft bokeh blobs); metallic origami cranes, each pulling a particle trail (at least five: the diving crane, two that cross and leave during the whip-tilt, then three converging on the centre from f3955); soft glowing rings; 4-point sparkle stars with glow (small far ones and a few large near ones), fixed in the space so they move with the camera; black space with a soft diagonal light band (lighter top-left and bottom-right quadrants, same pattern f3935-f3955).

**Typography:** none.
**3D:** metallic origami cranes (folded-paper facets with chrome-foil specular glints); they fly into depth toward a central vanishing point and shrink (template scale 1.45-1.48 -> 0.72-0.90); strong depth of field (the near crane's bokeh trail is soft); camera, measured on the star field (the stars stay put in the space and keep their brightness, so they are a usable reference): whip-tilt down f3938-f3957 that overtakes the diving crane, a decelerating tail with a slight pull-back to about f3972 (field rise 27.9% H, scale x0.88 from f3957), then from about f3976 a push-in that accelerates into the white flash (field scale x1.20 by f4000); the large near sparkles stream out 2.3-2.8x faster than the small far stars (depth parallax), so it is a 3D dolly or fly-through, not a flat zoom; the push's focus of expansion sits below the frame centre (fits 32-52% W, 61-75% H); lights come from the rings and the flash.
**Graphic elements and UI:** trails: fine particle column (top crane), chain of soft bokeh blobs (near crane), dotted particle streak (right crane); rings: luma 107-114 over an inner base of 26-30, FWHM 88-112 px (5.4-6.9% W); ring 3 luma 199 over 122, FWHM 144 px; sparkles: 4-point stars with a glow halo, small far ones and a few large near ones.
**Speed changes:** dive and whip-tilt accelerate (f3932-f3952), the tilt brakes hard between f3955 and f3957 and runs out over f3957-f3972 (field rise 4.4 -> 3.0 -> 1.3-1.5 -> 0.6 -> 0.03% H per frame) with a pull-back to x0.88; the camera reverses between f3972 and f3976 into a push-in that keeps accelerating into the flash (0.40 -> 0.71 -> 0.57 -> 0.81 -> 1.22% scale per frame over f3976-f3980 / f3980-f3985 / f3985-f3990 / f3990-f3995 / f3995-f4000); the convergence decelerates on screen (right crane 0.63 -> 0.25 -> 0.07% W per frame over f3965-f3976 / f3976-f3990 / f3990-f4000) with a near-stop at f3980-f3982 (0.03% W per frame) and a last push at 0.19-0.37% W per frame over f3982-f3992, then holds; relative to the star field it only slows (0.53-0.60 -> 0.30-0.33 -> 0.15-0.17% W per frame), so the on-screen near-stop and hold are the push-in carrying it outward; rings at constant speed, about 18 f apart; flash: slow start (3.6-4.6 luma per frame over f3997-f4002), then a steady 9-15 luma per frame up to full white at the cut (f4002-f4015). The unmasked frame median slows down at f4007-f4010 (160 -> 174) only because ring 3 leaves the frame then (the full-frame mean runs 24 luma above the ring-masked mean at f4007 and 7 at f4010); there is no hold in the flash itself.
**Transition out -> S26:** white flash, 26 frames (f4000-f4025): ring 3 blooms from the convergence point into a radial white-out with a cross-shaped glare (white coverage luma>150: 13% -> 100% at f4015); the picture is replaced under the white between f4012 and f4015 (c113); the new shot comes down from white while racking into focus (`v5.fx-exposure-recover`); shares: a full white frame, the centre-out light and the forward camera push (still accelerating at f4000: 1.22% scale per frame over f3995-f4000; UNCERTAIN whether it goes on under the white, the stars are lost after f4002); curve: curves/v5.trans-flash-white.csv; detail: detail/soft_4000-4025.
**Sound sync:** first trail f3940 on beat and strong onset f3940 (0); ring 1 birth f3964 = beat f3967 -3; ring 2 birth f3983 = half-beat f3981.5 +1; flash start f4000 = beat f3996 +4; cut under white f4013-f4015 = half-beat f4012.5 +1 to +3; push-in start about f3976 = beat f3967 +9 (off the grid, between the ring 1 and ring 2 births).
**Curves:** `curves/v5.3d-origami-converge.csv` bezier(0.443, 1.000, 0.747, 0.662) D=27, built 27 f = measured 27 f, rmse 0.013 (recommended); spring {91.6, 79.01, mass 1} built 36 f, rmse 0.072. Re-tracked on the crane's folded shape only (track.py template, ref f3980, bbox 944 484 96 132, scale step 0.01: scratch/v5c6_craneC_r2.csv); pictures without a lock (score < 0.6: f3960, f3962, f3985) are left out (scratch/v5c6_craneC_r2_locked.csv). The old track used two unlocked pictures (f3985 at 62.145% W, score 0.598, where ring 2 crosses the crane; f4000, score 0.571), which made the stall. This curve is the on-screen position, so it includes the camera (tilt tail, pull-back, push-in); the crane relative to the star field is in scratch/v5c6_craneC_starframe.csv (scratch/v5c6_tools/crane_starframe.py). `curves/v5.trans-flash-white.csv` bezier(0.471, 0.177, 0.000, -0.070) D=18, built 18 f = measured 18 f, rmse 0.019 (recommended); spring {215.1, 30.67} built 24 f, rmse 0.256 (a spring cannot ease in). Measured on the frame mean with the speaker icon and an annulus of +-144 px (ring-3 FWHM) around ring 3's least-squares radius (born f4000.2 at 811,459, 94.2 px per frame) excluded: scratch/v5c6_flash_ringmasked.csv. `curves/v5.cam-push-in-accelerate.i2.csv` bezier(1.000, 0.758, 0.848, 0.687) D=22, built 22 f against 24 f measured (f3976-f4000), rmse 0.026 (recommended); spring {69.6, 23.00} rmse 0.290. Samples: cumulative star-field scale (scratch/v5c6_starfield_fit.csv, made by scratch/v5c6_tools/starfield_fit.py: per picture pair a scale about the frame centre plus a shift, fitted to 6-29 matched stars; f3987, where ring 2 crosses the field, is not a chain link); progress 1 is f4000, the last picture with enough stars, not a settle. Per-star tracks: scratch/v5c6_star_tracks.csv (scratch/v5c6_tools/star_tracks.py).
**Uncertain:**
- UNCERTAIN: f3932-f3942 (crane dive curve) because 4 pictures and the crane leaves the frame; not fitted
- UNCERTAIN: f3964-f4007 (ring expansion curves) because linear expansion that leaves the frame without settling; least-squares lines reported instead of fitted curves
- UNCERTAIN: f3957-f4000 (camera move or star field moving; push-in focus of expansion; tail values) because a camera move and a star field moving toward a still camera look the same (the parallax only shows it is 3D); the focus of expansion (fits 32-52% W, 61-75% H) and the f3957-f3960 tail values (4 matched stars) are rough; single pairs scatter (f3987-f3990, where ring 2 crosses the field), so other matchings give x1.18-1.23 at f4000 instead of x1.20; crane positions relative to the field treat a crane like a field star (exact for the tilt, approximate for the push, which moves nearer objects more)

| technique | category | segments | reference frames | curve | summary |

| `v5.text-caption-word-fade` | text | S01, S06, S09, S12, S16, S19 | f1127-f1160, f27-f67, f2302-f2338, f2865-f2895 | curves/v5.text-caption-word-fade.csv, curves/v5.text-caption-word-fade.i2.csv, curves/v5.text-caption-word-fade.i3.csv, curves/v5.text-caption-word-fade.i4.csv | kinetic caption reveal: the letters sweep in left to right, each going from blurred and transparent to sharp white (1.2-2 f between letter starts, 10-25 f per letter, 30-40 f per word or line envelope); the lines of a caption start 8-21 f apart, following the voice-over rather than the beat, while the block drifts (`v5.text-caption-drift`) |
| `v5.text-caption-drift` | text | S01, S06, S09, S12, S16, S19 | f53-f72, f1140-f1160, f1700-f1735, f2758-f2820 | - | the whole caption block slides at a constant velocity for its whole life (already moving on its first picture, still moving on its last), away from the direction its lines step: left-side captions move left (-0.10 to -0.37 %W per frame, 15 px per 24 fps picture at the fastest) and mostly up (up to -0.40 %H per frame; one drifts down +0.11 %H, S09), right-side captions move right (+0.15 to +0.33 %W) and up (-0.10 to -0.35 %H); one S19 caption also grows x1.33 with a footage push-in (attachment UNCERTAIN) |
| `v5.text-caption-line-fade-out` | text | S01, S06, S09, S12, S16, S19 | f2338-f2350, f67-f92, f1213-f1230, f2897-f2927 | curves/v5.text-caption-line-fade-out.csv, curves/v5.text-caption-line-fade-out.i2.csv, curves/v5.text-caption-line-fade-out.i3.csv, curves/v5.text-caption-line-fade-out.i4.csv | caption lines leave one at a time in reading order, each as one unit fading and defocusing in place while the block keeps drifting: 8.5-19 f per line (30 f in S19), lines 8-16 f apart; long captions roll first in first out (each line lives 40-60 f) |

## Motion personality
Nothing in this video holds still: captions over the footage drift at a constant 4-6 px per frame from their first picture to their last while their letters blur in and their lines fade out in reading order, held titles and layouts creep or recede linearly, and between moves the camera keeps a slow drift, so the only true stops are the cuts. The graphic beats are dense UI and title sequences on near-black, staged as interactions (chips and chosen menu rows dip to about x0.8 and spring back, buttons squash under a gliding or diving cursor, a ring of glowing square tiles bursts out) and carried by a hard-working camera: one-picture snap zooms, whip pans, whip-tilts and reveal pull-backs with strong in-out eases over 17-46 frames (bezier x1 0.7-0.94), and accelerating push-ins that run straight into the next cut. Type and cards arrive as streaks from opposite edges (motion-blurred, chrome or liquid) and mostly land in 10-20 frames with stiff springs or hard ease-outs, headline pairs morph into counter-tapered pseudo-perspective as they land with sparkle-headed light streaks parking at their ends, and transitions favour light and depth (a star split, a radial zoom-through, a melt into a point flare, a white flash) over plain dissolves.
